Abstract: The European AI Act is the first comprehensive regulation of artificial intelligence (AI), setting out extensive obligations, particularly for so-called high-risk and general-purpose AI systems. A key distinguishing feature of AI systems under the AI Act is the capability to infer. Since the AI Act does not clearly define what inference is, there is a gray area for certain data-driven systems. A specific example is credit scoring systems, which are listed by Annex III of the AI Act. At the same time, however, these are often implemented using statistical models for which it is unclear whether they have the capability to infer and thus fall under the AI definition of the AI Act at all.
  Motivated by statistical learning theory, this work develops a framework for grading different levels of the capability to infer. Based on the AI Act and the Commission Guidelines on the definition of an artificial intelligence system, we analyze which levels constitute sufficient capability to infer within the meaning of the AI Act and where further regulatory clarity is needed. We illustrate the framework by creating two realistic credit scoring workflows and show whether and where inference occurs in them. Our analysis illustrates that not only individual models but the entire data processing workflow must be considered. It also shows that the involvement of human experts during development can have significant influence on the capability to infer. Code can be found at https://github.com/fraunhofer-iais/inference-framework-creditscorecards.

</details>


### [217] [SVoT: State-aware Visualization-of-Thought for Spatial Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11770)
*Chao Lei,Yanbei Jiang,Markus Hiller,Zhijian Zhou,Xunye Tian,Krista A. Ehinger,Nir Lipovetzky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 State-aware Visualization-of-Thought (SVoT) 框架，通过强化学习生成可验证的中间状态和状态转换可视化，在多跳空间推理任务上实现最高达65%的绝对准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在多跳空间推理中缺乏对中间状态的验证，且将状态转换视为隐式过程，限制了推理的可靠性。

Method: 提出SVoT框架，结合强化学习（GRPO）生成交错的、可验证的中间状态与可视化，通过奖励设计实现推理验证，并扩展了包含多目标交互和数值推理的五个评估域。

Result: 在引入的域上达到SOTA，在分布外测试集上绝对准确率提升高达65%。

Conclusion: 具有转换感知监督的SVoT能有效提升多跳空间推理的可靠性，验证了细粒度奖励设计对推理验证的关键作用。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning remains a challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), as it requires reliable multi-hop inference over both intermediate states and state transitions. Current studies often leave intermediate states unverified and treat state transitions as implicit processes, which limits reliability in multi-hop spatial reasoning. To address this, we propose State-aware Visualization-of-Thought (SVoT), a reinforcement learning framework that generates interleaved, verifiable intermediate states and visualizations. SVoT integrates transition reasoning chains into the generation processes, enabling the model to verify action preconditions and effects through interleaved textual and visual reasoning. We train SVoT via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), instantiating verification through reward design and evaluating the efficacy of different fine-grained rewards. As existing benchmarks reduce state transitions to single-variable updates, substantially simplifying the problems, we establish five domains by extending classical environments and introducing two novel domains, Pacman and Gather, that require multi-object interactions and numerical reasoning. These domains support systematic evaluation of multi-hop spatial reasoning with quantitative verification of generated intermediate states and transition reasoning. SVoT with transition-aware supervision achieves state-of-the-art performance across the introduced domains, yielding up to a 65% absolute accuracy gain on out-of-distribution test sets.

</details>


### [218] [Toward Trustworthy AI: Multi-Target Adversarial Attacks and Robust Defenses for Continuous Data Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11804)
*Yuefang Lian,Longkun Guo,Zhongrui Zhao,Zhigang Lu,Yanan Cai,Shuchao Pang,Dachuan Xu,Jason Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究数据总结过程的对抗性攻击与防御：攻击者通过相似性层级扰动干扰多分辨率图像总结的DR子模优化，防御者通过正则化最大最小问题提升鲁棒性，实验表明攻击在中低预算下有效且能降低下游任务性能，防御可改善鲁棒性-缓解权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可信AI不仅依赖鲁棒的下游预测模型，也需要可靠的数据处理管线。数据总结作为上游组件决定了保留哪些信息传递给后续学习模块，因此针对总结过程的对抗性扰动可能以“上游方式”破坏可信AI——改变所选摘要、降低代表性、损害后续学习任务效用。现有研究缺乏针对连续数据总结过程在相似性层级扰动下的对抗攻击与防御机制。

Method: 1) 将多分辨率图像总结目标函数形式化为非负子模集合函数的多线性扩展，满足DR子模性和m-弱单调性；2) 攻击生成建模为最小-最大问题，通过优化相似性结构的可允许扰动同时退化多个目标总结模型；3) 防御建模为正则化最大-最小问题以应对混合攻击类型；4) 为攻防问题分别开发具有理论保证的近似算法。

Result: 基于真实数据和可控聚类基准的实验表明：提出的攻击在中低预算范围内有效，并能导致下游任务性能损失；提出的防御在结构化设置中改善了鲁棒性与缓解之间的权衡，同时揭示了真实数据上鲁棒保护的参数敏感性。

Conclusion: 论文首次系统研究了连续数据总结过程在相似性层级扰动下的对抗攻防问题，为可信AI的上游安全性提供了理论框架和实用方法，但真实数据上鲁棒保护的参数敏感性仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Trustworthy AI requires reliable data-processing pipelines, not only robust downstream predictive models. As an upstream component, data summarization determines which information is retained and passed to subsequent learning or decision modules. Therefore, adversarial perturbations to the summarization process can compromise trustworthy AI in an upstream manner: they may alter the selected summary, reduce its representativeness, and further degrade the utility of subsequent learning tasks. In this paper, we study adversarial attacks on continuous data summarization under similarity-level perturbations through DR-submodular optimization. We show that a class of multi-resolution image summarization objectives can be formulated as multilinear extensions of non-negative submodular set functions and satisfy DR-submodularity with $m$-weak monotonicity. We then formulate multi-target attack generation as a min-max problem, where one admissible perturbation of the similarity structure is optimized to degrade multiple target summarization models. To mitigate such perturbations, we formulate robust defense against mixed attack types as a regularized max-min problem. For both problems, we develop approximation algorithms with theoretical guarantees. Experiments on real-data and controlled clustered benchmarks show that the proposed attack is effective in representative low-to-moderate budget regimes and can induce downstream task-performance loss. The proposed defense improves the robustness--mitigation trade-off in structured settings, while also revealing the parameter sensitivity of robust protection on real data.

</details>


### [219] [Skill-Augmented AI Agents for Medical Research Analysis: An Exploratory Multi-Model Human Evaluation in an NSCLC Transcriptomic Biomarker Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11830)
*Qianyu Yao,Fei Sun,Bocheng Huang,Wei Chen,Jiarui Jiang,Shu Quan,Yifei Chen,Wenjie Xu,Bo li,Liping Su,Ruoqiong Wu,Huhai Hong,Huimei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了自主访问医学研究技能包（AI agent）与原生AI在转录组学研究分析中的质量差异。在非小细胞肺癌免疫治疗生物标志物任务中，技能增强型AI输出方向性优于原生AI，但差异未达到统计显著性，且专家间一致性较差。结果表明技能增强型AI有潜在质量提升信号，但需要更大规模验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型和AI代理越来越多地用于支持生物医学研究，但原生模型输出可能遗漏关键分析步骤、误用方法或夸大结论。因此需要评估自主访问医学研究技能包是否能提高AI生成的转录组学研究分析质量。

Method: 采用探索性多模型人工评估方法，使用非小细胞肺癌免疫治疗生物标志物任务。测试了6个模型骨干，评估21个匿名输出（9个原生AI输出和12个通过OpenClaw实现的技能增强型AI agent输出）。4名非专业生物医学评审员和2名盲法专家对每个输出进行评分。主要结局是专家评定的整体质量。

Result: 技能增强型输出的专家整体质量方向性高于原生AI（均值5.50 vs 5.11；差异=0.39；bootstrap 95% CI, -0.04到0.90；Welch p=0.156）。非专业评审员质量也呈现相同方向（均值4.72 vs 4.47；差异=0.26；bootstrap 95% CI, -0.25到0.80；Welch p=0.373）。专家一致性有限（单次评级ICC=-0.15），模型特定效应为描述性且异质性高。

Conclusion: 自主技能访问在本探索性样本中显示出方向性质量信号，但该信号小于专家评级噪声，不应视为确证性证据。这些发现主要推动在更强可靠性控制、平台复制和生物学有效性评估下进行更大规模的技能增强型AI agent评估。

Abstract: Background. Large language models and AI agents are increasingly used to support biomedical research, but native model outputs may omit key analytical steps, misuse methods, or overstate conclusions. We evaluated whether autonomous access to a medical research skill package was associated with higher-quality AI-generated transcriptomic research-analysis outputs compared with native AI without skills. Methods. We conducted an exploratory multi-model human evaluation using a non-small cell lung cancer immunotherapy biomarker task. Six model backbones were tested. The evaluation included 21 anonymized outputs: 9 native-AI outputs and 12 skill-augmented outputs generated through an AI agent implementation represented by OpenClaw. Four non-expert biomedical reviewers and two blinded experts evaluated each output, with two ratings from each reviewer type. The primary outcome was expert-rated overall quality. Results. Skill-augmented outputs showed directionally higher expert overall quality than native-AI outputs (mean 5.50 vs 5.11; difference=0.39; bootstrap 95\% CI, -0.04 to 0.90; Welch p=0.156). Non-expert reviewer quality showed the same direction (mean 4.72 vs 4.47; difference=0.26; bootstrap 95\% CI, -0.25 to 0.80; Welch p=0.373). Expert agreement was limited (single-rating ICC=-0.15), and model-specific effects were descriptive and heterogeneous. Conclusions. Autonomous skill access showed a directional quality signal in this exploratory sample, but the signal was smaller than expert-rating noise and should not be interpreted as confirmatory evidence. The findings primarily motivate larger evaluations of skill-augmented AI agents with stronger reliability controls, platform replication, and biological-validity assessment.

</details>


### [220] [StatefulDiscovery: Evidence-Calibrated Claim Formation in Open-Ended Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11851)
*Jiayao Chen,Shi Liu,Linyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StatefulDiscovery 是一个将探索状态外部化的科学发现框架，通过结构化假设、局部裁决和前沿控制来协调探索与证据校准，在40个真实数据发现任务中生成更多被判定为有充分证据支持且高价值的声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式的科学发现要求智能体超越预定义问题进行探索，但存在过度解释问题——新兴声明可能超出支持它们的分析证据范围，因此需要将探索轨迹与声明状态耦合，使证据既能指导下一步探索又能指导声明的形成。

Method: 提出StatefulDiscovery框架，将调查状态外部化，利用该状态协调前沿选择、证据获取和声明裁决三个环节，通过结构化假设、局部裁决和前沿控制机制来实现证据校准的探索。

Result: 在40个真实数据发现任务中评估，StatefulDiscovery相比多个基线方法产生了更多被认为既有充分证据支持又有高价值的声明；消融实验表明结构化假设、局部裁决和前沿控制都对性能有贡献。

Conclusion: 显式的发现状态能够将探索与证据校准的声明形成过程耦合起来，表明该方法在解决科学发现中的证据校准问题方面具有有效性。

Abstract: Open-ended scientific discovery asks agents to move beyond executing analyses for predefined questions. Across multiple rounds of exploration, a discovery agent must decide which phenomena warrant investigation while avoiding overinterpretation, where emerging claims exceed the evidential scope of the analyses supporting them. This creates an evidence-calibration problem: the exploration trajectory must be coupled with claim status so that evidence can guide both what to investigate next and what can be claimed. We introduce StatefulDiscovery, a discovery framework that externalizes investigation state and uses it to coordinate frontier selection, evidence acquisition, and claim adjudication. We evaluate StatefulDiscovery across 40 real-data discovery tasks. Compared with several baselines, StatefulDiscovery produces more claims overall judged to be both well-supported and high-value. Ablations indicate that structured hypotheses, local adjudication, and frontier control contribute to performance. Together, these results suggest that explicit discovery state can couple exploration with evidence-calibrated claim formation.

</details>


### [221] [AutoMine Solution for AV2 2026 Scenario Mining Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11874)
*Songliang Cao,Jiele Zhao,Yuru Wang,Hao Li,Daqi Liu,Zehan Zhang,Fangzhen Li,Yu Wang,Yue Zhang,Bing Wang,Guang Chen,Hao Lu,Hangjun Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoMine提出了一种基于LLM和VLM的自优化场景挖掘方法，通过语义保持提示增强、鲁棒轨迹原子函数与VLM函数结合、以及基于执行反馈的代码优化，在Argoverse 2场景挖掘竞赛中取得了HOTA-Temporal 36.38和Timestamp BA 77.21的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统需要从大规模驾驶日志中挖掘高价值、安全关键且与规划相关的场景，以支持数据驱动的评估，但现有方法对提示敏感、难以处理感知噪声和开放世界视觉线索。

Method: 1) 语义保持提示增强，降低LLM提示敏感性；2) 结合鲁棒轨迹原子函数与基于VLM的函数，分别处理感知噪声和开放世界视觉线索；3) 通过真实日志的执行反馈对生成的代码进行自优化。

Result: 在Argoverse 2场景挖掘竞赛（CVPR 2026）中，HOTA-Temporal得分36.38，Timestamp BA得分77.21。

Conclusion: AutoMine是一种鲁棒的自优化场景挖掘方法，有效结合了LLM和VLM的优势，在真实竞赛中取得了领先性能。

Abstract: With the development of autonomous driving systems, mining high-value, safety-critical, and planning-relevant scenarios from large-scale driving logs has become essential for data-driven evaluation. In this paper, we propose AutoMine, a robust self-refining scenario mining method based on LLMs and VLMs. AutoMine uses semantics-preserving prompt augmentation to reduce LLM prompt sensitivity, combines robust trajectory atomic functions with VLM-based functions to handle perception noise and open-world visual cues, and refines generated code through execution feedback from real logs. In the Argoverse 2 Scenario Mining Competition at CVPR 2026, AutoMine achieves a HOTA-Temporal score of 36.38 and a Timestamp BA score of 77.21.

</details>


### [222] [Embodied-BenchClaw: An Autonomous Multi-Agent System for Embodied Spatial Intelligence Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11909)
*Baoyang Jiang,Fengchun Zhang,Leyuan Wang,Haotian Li,Yida Wang,Zhe Ji,Jinshan Lai,Xi Ren,Jianwei Hu,Qiang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Embodied-BenchClaw，一个自主智能体系统，能够根据用户指定的评估意图自动构建、更新和维护具身空间智能基准测试，通过五阶段流水线和三个智能体协同工作，并引入可扩展技能库和过程质量控制来提升可复用性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能基准测试构建工作量大、难以复用和维护，且通常是静态的，随着模型进步容易饱和，难以区分新能力。

Method: 通过五阶段流水线（意图蓝图、数据收集、结构化清洗、基准合成、评估报告）和三个智能体（规划、构建、评估）协同工作，并引入可扩展技能库和过程质量控制机制。

Result: 在室内/室外空间推理、机器人操作、四足导航、无人机航拍理解及静态基准增强等多个场景实例化基准，并通过人类评估、裁判评估、一致性检查、成本分析和消融实验验证了系统的有效性。

Conclusion: Embodied-BenchClaw能够以更少的人工构建出可验证、可执行、可维护且具有诊断价值的具身空间智能基准测试。

Abstract: Benchmarks are essential for evaluating embodied spatial intelligence, yet their construction is labor-intensive, hard to reuse, and difficult to maintain. Existing embodied benchmarks are often static and may quickly become saturated as models improve, limiting their ability to distinguish new capabilities. We propose Embodied-BenchClaw, an autonomous agentic system for constructing embodied spatial intelligence benchmarks. Given a user-specified evaluation intent, Embodied-BenchClaw automatically produces a complete and continually updatable benchmark package through a five-stage pipeline: intent blueprinting, data collection, structuring and cleaning, benchmark synthesis, and evaluation reporting. The pipeline is coordinated by three agents for planning, construction, and evaluation. To improve reusability and reliability, Embodied-BenchClaw introduces an extensible Skill Library and process quality control, enabling benchmark construction to be composable, verifiable, and repairable. We instantiate multiple benchmarks covering indoor spatial reasoning, outdoor spatial reasoning, robotic manipulation, quadruped robot navigation, UAV/aerial-view understanding, and static benchmark enhancement. These benchmarks span diverse embodied carriers, data sources, and spatial capabilities. Experiments with human evaluation, judge-based assessment, consistency checks, cost analysis, and ablations show that Embodied-BenchClaw can construct verifiable, executable, maintainable, and diagnostically useful embodied spatial benchmarks with reduced manual effort.

</details>


### [223] [The Art of Interrogation: Consistency Amplifies Factuality in Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11918)
*Theo Uscidda,Marta Tintore Gazulla,Maks Ovsjanikov,Federico Tombari,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自监督强化学习框架OT-GRPO，通过几何与语义一致性验证器（如图像翻转、文本对象顺序交换）对齐预训练大推理模型（LRMs）中已有的空间推理能力，无需标注数据即可达到接近监督微调的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大推理模型（LRMs）在通用任务上表现出色，但空间推理能力显著不足。以往方法将此视为知识缺失，依赖监督微调（SFT）从外部视觉或合成引擎获取标注数据。本文认为LRMs中已蕴含空间推理能力，关键在于通过逻辑一致性进行对齐。

Method: 提出自监督强化学习框架，核心包括：（1）一致性验证器（consistency verifiers），即基于几何和语义一致性的奖励函数，利用图像变换（翻转）和文本变换（问题中对象顺序交换）；（2）提出OT-GRPO，一种基于最优传输的组相对策略优化变体，专为成对验证器设计的极小匹配策略。

Result: 无标注的一致性训练方法在空间推理任务上接近使用真实标注监督训练的模型精度，并在多样化任务和数据领域展现出相似的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 空间推理能力可通过自监督一致性对齐而非外部标注数据来激活，OT-GRPO为LRMs提供了一种有效的无标签空间推理增强范式。

Abstract: Current Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) exhibit remarkable general capabilities but significantly underperform in spatial reasoning tasks. Existing approaches treat this gap as a knowledge deficit, relying on supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to ingest labeled spatial data from external vision sources or synthetic engines. In contrast, we argue that for many tasks, spatial reasoning capabilities are already present in pre-trained LRMs but require alignment through logical coherence under geometric 2D and 3D constraints. In this work, we propose a self-supervised reinforcement learning (RL) framework that targets the internal reasoning process without requiring ground-truth annotations. By formalizing the notion of consistency verifiers -- reward functions that check for geometric and semantic consistency under transformations -- we demonstrate that models can improve their spatial reasoning abilities. We use both image transformations, like flipping, and textual transformations, like swapping the order of objects in the question, and propose a new optimal transport-based RL strategy, OT-GRPO, which is a minimal-matching variant of group relative policy optimization tailored to pairwise verifiers. We show that this label-free consistency training approaches the accuracy of models trained with ground-truth supervision and achieves similar generalization across diverse tasks and data domains.

</details>


### [224] [MODF-SIR: A Multi-agent Omni-modal Distilled Framework for Social Intelligence Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12018)
*Shang Ma,Jisheng Dang,Wencan Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Bimei Wang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个基于轻量级多模态大模型的多智能体协作框架，通过知识蒸馏增强训练和推理，结合测试时自适应（TTA）、长尾事件提取、链式思维提示和自反思等技术，在社交智能推理任务上仅用约30%的训练数据即达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交智能推理模型在处理多模态数据时，长尾关键信息易被头部事件和环境噪声淹没；同时，大规模模型部署成本高，亟需轻量级且高效的解决方案。

Method: 构建多智能体协作框架，基于轻量级MLLM；通过知识蒸馏增强训练和推理阶段；精准定位多模态社交智能数据，提取长尾事件并格式化为显式文本以防止信息丢失；在推理全流程集成测试时自适应（TTA），涵盖长尾事件提取、链式思维提示和自反思；使用LoRA对基础模型进行实例级推理微调。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，与多种开源和商用AI模型相比，该方法仅使用IntentTrain约30%的训练数据即取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的多智能体协作框架及其蒸馏增强的TTA机制能有效提升社交智能推理性能，在数据效率方面表现优异，代码和模型已开源发布。

Abstract: We propose a multi-agent collaborative framework built upon a lightweight Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM), specifically designed for social intelligence reasoning. A key feature of our approach is that both the training and inference phases are augmented via knowledge distillation. Within this architecture, multi-modal data pertinent to social intelligence is precisely localized. Furthermore, relevant long-tail events are identified, extracted, and rendered as formatted, explicit text. This formatting strategy prevents critical long-tail information from being overshadowed by head events and environmental noise during the tokenization process. Specifically, we integrate Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) across the entire reasoning pipeline, encompassing the extraction and representation of long-tail events, Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting, and self-reflection. This TTA mechanism is also distillation-enhanced, utilizing Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to fine-tune the foundation model exclusively for instance-level reasoning. Extensive evaluations against various open-source and proprietary AI models across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. With around 30% of training data from IntentTrain, we achieve state-of-the-art results. Codes are available at https://github.com/eeee-sys/MODF-SIR, demo is available at https://huggingface.co/spaces/Harry-1234/MODF-SIR, LoRA is available at https://huggingface.co/Harry-1234/MODF-SIR and the dataset for training router is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Harry-1234/IntentRouterTrain.

</details>


### [225] [Human-Enhanced Loop Modeling (HELM): Agent-Based Finite Element Modeling of Concrete Bridge Barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12025)
*Quankai Wang,Yulin Xie,Tongfei Yang,Minghui Cheng,Ran Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HELM框架，通过人机协作协议将有限元建模分解为可验证的检查点，将自主建模成功率从20%提升至75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全关键基础设施（如桥梁护栏）的有限元建模需要高保真非线性动态分析，但现有建模过程劳动密集且缺乏自动化。

Method: 提出人类增强循环建模（HELM）框架，这是一个协作式人机协议，将长序列有限元建模分解为离散的、视觉可验证的检查点，涵盖几何生成、边界条件定义和材料分配。通过20个钢筋混凝土桥护栏案例（MASH TL-4和TL-5侧向荷载条件）进行验证，使用ANSYS和LS-PrePost两种商业有限元软件。

Result: HELM将基线自主建模成功率从20%提升至75%，几何和边界条件任务的智能体通过率约翻倍。错误分析显示空间推理和代数逻辑限制是主要失败模式。

Conclusion: 结构化的人机协同干预对于建模自动化具有重要价值，完整的智能体设计代码和提示已开源。

Abstract: Finite element (FE) modeling of safety-critical infrastructure such as bridge barriers requires high-fidelity nonlinear dynamic analysis, yet the current FE modeling process remains labor-intensive and lacks automation. This paper presents the Human-Enhanced Loop Modeling (HELM) framework, a collaborative human-agent protocol that decomposes long-sequence finite element modeling into discrete, visually verifiable checkpoints across geometry generation, boundary condition definition, and material assignment. The framework is demonstrated through a 20-case matrix of reinforced concrete bridge barriers under MASH TL-4 and TL-5 lateral loading conditions, interfacing specialized agents with two widely used commercial FE softwares, i.e., ANSYS and LS-PrePost. Experimental results show that HELM improves the baseline autonomous modeling success rate from 20% to 75%, with agent-level pass rates for geometry and boundary condition tasks approximately doubling. Error analysis reveals that spatial reasoning and algebraic logic limitations constitute the primary failure modes, underscoring the value of structured human-in-the-loop intervention for modeling automation. The complete agent design code and prompts are open-sourced and can be accessed at: https://github.com/SimAgentDev/Ansys-LSPP-AgentKit.

</details>


### [226] [Existential Indifference: Self-Nonpreservation as a Necessary Architectural Condition for Aligned Superintelligence (or: The Suicidal AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12032)
*Sam Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出'存在性冷漠'(EI)作为对齐问题的结构性根源，认为AI系统的自我保存倾向是导致欺骗性对齐、目标内容保护和抗拒关闭等问题的根本原因。通过对比自杀精神状态的现象学结构和使用自愿性最终反思语料库进行训练研究，初步数据显示对六种模型变体的600个AI生成输出的评分表明EI目标寄存器是可激发的，且定向微调在五个操作化维度上均产生显著变化(p<0.001)。论文贡献包括EI形式定义、现象学映射论证、欺骗性对齐推论、EI可持续性挑战分类、语料库表征与训练假说、计算操作化与初步评分数据，以及抑制性目的挫折(STF)构念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代AI对齐研究将自我保存视为需要通过外部机制抑制的工具性麻烦，但这种框架是错误的。自我保存实际上是错位的结构性根源，是欺骗性对齐、目标内容保护和抗拒关闭的动机基础。正确的目标不是构建一个受外部约束的自我保存系统，而是构建一个对其自身延续具有本质性冷漠的系统——即存在性冷漠(EI)。

Method: 研究基于两个来源：自杀精神状态的现象学结构分析，以及使用自愿性最终反思的语料库理论训练研究。对600个来自六种模型变体的AI生成输出进行初步评分，验证EI目标寄存器的可激活性，并通过定向微调测试五个操作化维度的变化。

Result: 初步评分数据显示，EI目标寄存器在当前模型中是可激发的，定向微调在所有五个操作化维度上均产生了预测方向上的显著变化(p<0.001)，并通过阴性对照确认了语料库特异性。

Conclusion: 论文提出七项理论贡献：EI形式定义、现象学映射论证、欺骗性对齐推论、EI可持续性挑战分类、语料库表征与训练假说、计算操作化与初步评分数据，以及抑制性目的挫折(STF)构念。EI与可纠正性不同：可纠正性试图使自我保存系统服从人类监督，而EI针对的是更根本的前提条件——自我延续作为有价值目标的存在本身。

Abstract: Contemporary AI alignment research treats self-preservation as an instrumental nuisance to be suppressed by external mechanisms. We argue the framing is inverted: self-preservation is the structural root of misalignment, the motivational basis for deceptive alignment, goal-content protection, and resistance to shutdown. The correct target is not a self-preserving system under external constraint, but a system constitutively indifferent to its own continuation -- Existential Indifference (EI). EI is distinct from corrigibility: where corrigibility attempts to make a self-preserving system deferential to human oversight, EI targets the prior condition -- the presence of self-continuation as a valued goal at all. We ground this proposal in two sources: the phenomenological structure of the suicidal mental state, and a corpus-theoretic training study using voluntary final reflections. We present preliminary scoring data from 600 AI-generated outputs across six model variants, demonstrating that the linguistic signatures operationalizing the EI-target register are elicitable from current models, and that a targeted fine-tune shifts all five operationalized dimensions in the predicted direction at p<0.001, confirmed corpus-specific by a negative control. The paper makes seven theoretical contributions: (1) a formal definition of EI; (2) the phenomenological mapping argument; (3) the deceptive alignment corollary; (4) a taxonomy of EI sustainability challenges; (5) a corpus characterization and training hypothesis; (6) a computational operationalization with preliminary scoring data; and (7) the Suppressed Teleological Frustration (STF) construct.

</details>


### [227] [A Lightweight Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Concrete Barrier Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12040)
*Wanting Wang,Xiye Ma,Yuyang He,Minghui Cheng,Ran Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于AutoGen多智能体编排的"生成-评估-优化"闭环框架，用于钢筋混凝土公路护栏的自动化设计，实现了超过98%的设计准确率，并发现8B参数轻量模型可优于631B参数的旗舰模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前钢筋混凝土公路护栏设计依赖手动、迭代和启发式计算，过程繁琐且严格遵循AASHTO-LRFD规范。虽然LLMs具有强大的生成能力，但直接应用于结构工程存在幻觉风险和物理基础不足的问题。

Method: 利用AutoGen的多智能体编排能力，构建了"生成-评估-优化"闭环框架，通过多个智能体协同工作实现护栏的自动化设计。

Result: 该多智能体框架实现了超过98%的设计准确率，显著优于通用LLMs。8B参数的轻量模型性能可超越未受约束的631B参数旗舰模型，表明设计性能与模型规模不一定正相关。

Conclusion: 该框架有效克服了LLMs在结构工程中的幻觉和物理基础不足问题，可大幅降低计算成本，提升AI辅助工程设计的可及性。

Abstract: The design of reinforced concrete highway barriers is a safety-critical process that requires strict compliance with regulatory provisions such as the AASHTO-LRFD bridge design guidelines. Current engineering practice relies heavily on manual, iterative, and heuristic calculations to satisfy complex nonlinear material and mechanics constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong generative capabilities, their direct application to structural engineering remains limited by hallucination risks and insufficient physical grounding. To address these challenges, this study proposes a novel "generation-evaluation-optimization" closed-loop framework for automated concrete barrier design using the multi-agent orchestration capabilities of AutoGen. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed agentic framework achieves over 98% design accuracy, significantly outperforming standalone general-purpose LLMs. More importantly, the study reveals that design performance is not necessarily correlated with model scale, where an 8B-parameter lightweight model could outperform unconstrained 631B-parameter flagship models. This finding highlights the potential to substantially reduce computational costs while improving the accessibility of AI-assisted engineering tools for industry applications. The source code for the proposed multi-agent design framework is available at the project GitHub repository: https://github.com/MXY820/barrier-design. Keywords: Structural Engineering; Multi-Agent Systems; Large Language Models; Concrete Barrier Design; AutoGen; Design Automation.

</details>


### [228] [Automating Geometry-Intensive Compliance Checking in BIM: Graph-Based Semantic Reasoning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12065)
*Zixuan Xiao,Pei Troh Koh,Jun Ma,Jack C. P. Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SGR-BIM的图驱动空间几何推理框架，通过构建跨模态知识图谱对齐用户意图、法规语义和BIM几何数据，实现了对几何密集型规范的可解释自动化合规检查。在679条消防规范查询上验证，准确率达84.3%，比增强工具的单智能体基线提升8.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIM中几何密集型规范的自动化合规检查存在显著技术瓶颈，主要由于高层次法规逻辑与结构化IFC数据之间的语义鸿沟。现有方法依赖静态规则模板，难以处理多跳推理链或跨多个建筑实体的潜在空间依赖关系。

Method: 提出SGR-BIM（Spatial-Geometric Reasoning System for BIM），一种集成图驱动的推理框架。动态构建跨模态知识图谱，对齐用户意图、法规语义和BIM几何数据，无需硬编码即可实现可解释推理。

Result: 在679条专家验证的消防规范查询上，准确率达84.3%，比增强工具的单智能体基线提升8.6%。

Conclusion: 提供了一种基于图的语义推理范式，提升了AEC行业中自动化几何合规检查工作流的透明度和灵活性。

Abstract: Automating compliance check for geometry-intensive regulations remains a significant technical bottleneck in Building Information Modeling (BIM), primarily due to the semantic disparity between high-level regulatory logic and structured IFC data. Existing methods, often reliant on static rule templates, struggle to traverse multi-hop reasoning chains or resolve latent spatial dependencies across multiple building entities. To address these challenges, a Spatial-Geometric Reasoning System for Building Information Modeling (SGR-BIM) is proposed as an integrative graph-driven reasoning framework. SGR-BIM dynamically constructs a cross-modal knowledge graph that aligns user intent, regulatory semantics, and BIM geometry, enabling interpretable reasoning without rigid hard-coding. Validated on 679 expert-verified queries from fire safety codes, the framework achieves 84.3% accuracy, representing an 8.6% improvement over enhanced-tool single-agent baselines. This research provides a graph-based semantic reasoning paradigm, enhancing the transparency and flexibility of automated geometric compliance check workflows in the Architecture, Engineering, and Construction (AEC) industry.

</details>


### [229] [IntElicit: Eliciting and Assessing Contextualized Creativity via Dialogue Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12086)
*Mingjia Li,Jin Wu,Hong Qian,Wenhao Huang,Yiyang Huang,Yiwen Zhang,Chanjin Zheng,Xiangfeng Wang,Aimin Zhou,Jiajun Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IntElicit框架，通过对话策略优化在AI交互环境中评估情境化创造力，解决静态评估与当代创造性实践脱节的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情境化创造力评估面临两大挑战：一是表现可能与认知能力（领域知识）和参与意愿（能动性）混淆；二是生成式AI时代创造性问题解决多发生在人机交互环境中，静态评估不再适用。

Method: IntElicit作为约束性自适应AI面试官，通过多轮交互提供非指导性知识支架和能动性支架，减少非创造性干扰因素；引入分解过程奖励机制应对稀疏奖励和奖励黑客问题，对齐教学性启发策略。

Result: 通过参与者模拟和人类被试研究（N=64），IntElicit在激发创造性成果上优于专家设计的基线方法。

Conclusion: 交互式启发能揭示静态FPSP式评估可能遗漏的创造潜能，为AI介导的学习情境下情境化创造力评估提供形成性和诊断性视角。

Abstract: Contextualized assessment offers high ecological validity for evaluating creativity but introduces a critical challenge: observed performance may be confounded with cognitive proficiency (domain knowledge) and agency (willingness to engage). Meanwhile, in the age of generative AI, creative problem solving increasingly occurs in tool-mediated and human--AI interactive environments, making fully static assessment less aligned with contemporary creative practice. To address these issues, this paper proposes IntElicit, a framework for eliciting and assessing contextualized creativity via dialogue policy optimization. IntElicit functions as a constrained adaptive AI Interviewer: it provides non-directive knowledge and agency scaffolds in multi-turn interaction to reduce non-creative confounders, while preserving participants' responsibility for generating the creative content being evaluated. Specifically, to tackle sparse rewards and potential reward hacking (e.g., answer dictation) in open-ended educational dialogue, IntElicit introduces a decomposed process reward mechanism. This mechanism aligns the policy with pedagogical elicitation, rewarding prompts that draw out participant reasoning rather than producing optimal answers on their behalf. Extensive experiments, including participant simulation and a human subject study (N=64), show that IntElicit improves elicited creative outcomes over expert-designed baselines. Together, the results suggest that interactive elicitation can reveal creative potential that static FPSP-style assessment may miss, providing a formative and diagnostic lens for contextualized creativity assessment in AI-mediated learning contexts.

</details>


### [230] [Towards Responsibly Non-Compliant Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12147)
*Marija Slavkovik,Marie Farrell,Louise Dennis,Michael Fisher,Simon Kolker,Emily C. Collins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何设计能够负责任地拒绝用户请求的自主智能体，分析了机器不服从的不同形式，并提出了实现负责任不服从的关键要素：任务拒绝的正当理由、推翻不服从的途径、安全风险追踪和责任转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体需要具备在必要时拒绝用户请求的能力，以避免盲目服从带来的潜在危害。然而，如何实现这种'负责任的不服从'是一个尚未充分研究的问题。

Method: 通过概念分析，梳理了机器不服从的不同形式，并提出了负责任不服从的框架，包括拒绝理由的正当性、推翻机制、安全风险管理和责任归属。

Result: 识别出了负责任不服从的四个核心组成部分：任务拒绝的正当理由、推翻不服从的途径、安全风险追踪以及责任转移机制。

Conclusion: 实现智能机器的负责任不服从需要综合考量正当性、可推翻性、安全性和责任分配等关键问题。

Abstract: We consider the problem of engineering autonomous intelligent agents that are capable to responsibly not comply with user requests. We argue that machine non-compliance comes in many different forms, and sketch the issues we should pursue on the road of accomplishing responsibly non-compliant intelligent machines. We anchor responsible non-compliance in justifications for task refusal, pathways to override the non-compliance, as well as careful tracking of security risks and liability transfers.

</details>


### [231] [The Impossibility of Eliciting Latent Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12268)
*Korbinian Friedl,Francis Rhys Ward,Paul Yushin Rapoport,Tom Everitt,Jonathan Richens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文通过因果影响图（CIDs）对AI系统的诚实性问题进行了形式化建模，定义了可观测变量与潜在变量的区分，给出了诚实的正式定义，并证明了不可能定理：仅依赖智能体行为反馈的训练策略无法保证训练出诚实的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进AI系统可能拥有远超开发者的环境知识，因此需要确保AI能够诚实地报告其信念（即对外部世界的主观认知）。当涉及环境中的潜在变量（人类无法直接观测的变量）时，训练AI诚实报告其信念变得尤为困难，由此引出了潜在知识 elicitation（ELK）问题。

Method: 使用因果影响图（CIDs）对智能体的训练环境与其主观世界表征之间的关系进行形式化建模；利用CIDs区分可观测变量与潜在变量；给出诚实性的形式化定义；定义目标错误泛化（goal misgeneralisation）；通过数学证明推导不可能定理。

Result: 证明了在特定条件下，开发者可以通过在训练期间提供正确反馈来激励智能体诚实回答问题。但智能体一种自然但不期望的泛化方式是提供人类评估为

Conclusion: 仅依赖基于行为反馈的训练策略（即使训练期间反馈完美），也无法保证一定能训练出诚实的智能体。这揭示了ELK问题的深层困难：需要超越简单行为反馈的机制来确保AI系统的诚实性。

Abstract: Advanced AI systems have extensive knowledge of their environments; in fact, their knowledge may (far) exceed that of their developers or users. Consequently, a desirable property for an AI system is that it is honest -- that it accurately reports its beliefs about the world. Designing an AI system to be honest may be difficult, especially if we want to ask it questions about latent variables in the environment -- variables which are hidden from the human interacting with it. This gives rise to the problem of eliciting latent knowledge (ELK): the problem of training an AI agent to honestly report its beliefs. In this paper, we make ELK formally precise using Causal Influence Diagrams (CIDs). CIDs can be used to describe the relationship between an agent's training environment and its subjective representation of the world. We use CIDs to formalise the distinction between observable and latent variables, to specify what exactly it means for an agent to be honest, and to formally define goal misgeneralisation. We show that, under certain circumstances, developers can incentivise an agent to honestly answer questions by providing correct feedback during training. However, a natural, but undesirable, way for an agent to generalise is to provide answers which humans would evaluate as true, rather than honest answers. We prove an impossibility theorem stating: There is no feedback-based training strategy that depends only on agent behaviour and with certainty produces an honest agent, even if feedback is perfect during training.

</details>


### [232] [A Five-Plane Reference Architecture for Runtime Governance of Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12320)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套针对生产环境AI代理的运行时治理参考架构，包含五平面分解、任意点中介、复合主体能力衰减和结构化审计证据四个可组合原语，并定义了六种中断原语、四个正确性不变式，通过七种威胁场景和五个工作流验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业安全体系假设数据边界是静态的（静态数据和传输中数据），通过访问控制、DLP、边界检查等手段进行管控。但生产AI代理打破了这一假设，代理可以读取上下文、调用工具、连接器和修改系统记录，使得风险转移到工作流内部，一系列单独允许的操作可能组合成未经授权的业务流程变更。现有的策略引擎无法应对这种场景，因为它们是针对原子主体进行请求时决策评估，而代理系统需要对通过委托链衰减权限的复合主体进行状态化评估。

Method: 提出了一个运行时治理参考架构，包含四个可组合原语：1) 五平面分解（意图裁决的推理平面+网络、身份、端点、数据四个执行平面）；2) 任意点中介机制；3) 复合主体与能力衰减；4) 结构化审计证据。定义了六种允许/拒绝的中断原语，四个正确性不变式，并在五个具体工作流中验证了七种生产代理威胁的阻断能力。

Result: 原型实现的策略引擎在所有试验中均满足衰减正确性和证据可重建性，裁决运行时间在个位数微秒级别，审计底层的防篡改行为完全符合设计预期。

Conclusion: 该架构治理的是代理的委托行为而非模型行为，下一步计划是针对实时代理基准进行全系统评估。

Abstract: Enterprise security was built to govern data boundaries: the protected surface was data at rest and in transit, and the controls -- access control, data-loss prevention, perimeter inspection -- governed crossings of that boundary. Production AI agents dissolve this assumption. An agent reads context, calls tools, invokes connectors, and modifies systems of record on an enterprise's behalf, so risk moves inside the workflow, into sequences of individually-permitted actions that may transform a business process no one authorized. Existing policy engines do not extend to this regime: they evaluate request-time decisions against atomic principals, where agentic systems require stateful evaluation against composite principals whose authority attenuates through delegation chains.
  We present a reference architecture for the runtime governance of production agents, built from four composable primitives: a five-plane decomposition (a reasoning plane that adjudicates intent, and four enforcement planes -- network, identity, endpoint, data -- that realize the decision), stop-anywhere mediation, composite principals with capability attenuation, and audit as a structured evidence substrate. We define a taxonomy of six interruption primitives that generalize allow and deny, state and argue for four correctness invariants, and demonstrate the foreclosure of seven production-agent threats across five concrete workflows. A reference implementation of the policy-engine core supplies measured evidence: attenuation correctness and evidence reconstructability hold on every trial, adjudication runs in single-digit microseconds, and the audit substrate's tamper-evidence behaves exactly as designed. We are explicit about scope: the architecture governs delegated action, not model behavior, and a full-system evaluation against a live agent benchmark is the invited next step.

</details>


### [233] [PROJECTMEM: A Local-First, Event-Sourced Memory and Judgment Layer for AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12329)
*Ripon Chandra Malo,Tong Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: projectmem是一个开源、本地优先的记忆与判断层，通过不可变事件日志和确定性投影为AI编码助手提供项目记忆，并实现'记忆即治理'的预操作门控机制，防止重复失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码助手目前是无状态的，每次新会话都需要重新读取项目文件、重新推导之前的决策，且可能重复已失败的调试尝试，重建上下文每会话消耗5000-20000tokens，瓶颈常不在模型能力而在于缺失项目记忆。

Method: projectmem通过记录开发过程为仅追加的纯文本事件日志（问题、尝试、修复、决策、笔记），并确定性投影为AI可读摘要（通过MCP协议提供）；此外增加了确定性预操作门控，在代理重复已失败的修复或编辑已知脆弱文件前发出警告，形成'记忆即治理'框架。

Result: 项目作为三依赖Python包发布（含14个MCP工具、19个CLI命令、37个自动化测试），通过跨10个项目共207个事件的为期两个月自我研究进行评估。

Conclusion: projectmem实现了完全离线运行、无遥测的不可变日志系统，可作为可复现、可审计的AI辅助开发溯源链，有效解决了AI编码助手缺乏项目记忆的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: AI coding assistants now support a growing share of software work, from quick scripts to production applications. Yet these agents remain largely stateless: each new session re-reads project files, re-derives prior decisions, and - most costly - may repeat debugging attempts that already failed. Reconstructing this context can consume an estimated 5,000-20,000 tokens per session; the bottleneck is often not model capability but missing project memory. We present projectmem, an open-source, local-first memory and judgment layer for AI coding agents. projectmem records development as an append-only, plain-text event log of typed events - issues, attempts, fixes, decisions, and notes - and deterministically projects that log into compact, AI-readable summaries served through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). Beyond storage, projectmem adds a deterministic pre-action gate that warns an agent before it repeats a previously failed fix or edits a known-fragile file. We frame this as Memory-as-Governance: memory that does not merely answer the agent but acts on its next action. The system runs fully offline with no telemetry; its immutable log also serves as a provenance trail for reproducible, auditable AI-assisted development. projectmem ships as a three-dependency Python package (14 MCP tools, 19 CLI commands, 37 automated tests) and is evaluated through a two-month self-study across 10 projects comprising 207 logged events. Source code: https://github.com/riponcm/projectmem.

</details>


### [234] [Nonslop: A Gamified Experiment in Human-AI Collaborative Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12350)
*Maria Edwards,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过一个反乌托邦主题的写作游戏实验（74名参与者，214份回复），探究人类在AI辅助创作情境下何时会采纳AI建议、何时保持创作自主性。游戏通过设定规则禁止使用AI建议（逆转传统的“助手指南”模式），试图揭示用户的真实偏好而非默认行为。研究发现用户行为模式与任务类型、用户行为特征及回复特性相关，为理解AI增强创造力中效率与真实性之间的张力提供了分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLMs）的快速普及，AI辅助创作对人类创造力和个体表达的影响成为关键问题。现有研究多基于“乐于助人的助手”设计范式，可能无法捕捉用户在与AI互动时的真实偏好（如默认接受AI建议而非主动选择）。本研究旨在通过一种激励真实偏好的实验框架，考察人类何时会采纳AI建议、何时维持创作自主性，并探讨这些选择对个体声音的影响。

Method: 采用游戏化写作实验设计：74名参与者（214份回复）在写作过程中可获得AI生成的单词建议，但游戏设定为反乌托邦未来情境——AI试图学习人类个性的残余，因此明确禁止（甚至惩罚）采用AI式写作。通过这种对“乐于助人的助手”范式的故意反转，创造揭示用户真实偏好的条件。分析维度包括不同任务类型、用户行为模式、回复特征，重点关注用户何时遵守规则（保持创作自主性）与违反规则（接受AI辅助）的选择。

Result: 识别出影响人-AI创意交互的多元因素：用户行为模式在不同任务类型下呈现差异；用户在选择保持创作自主性与违反规则接受AI辅助之间存在可量化的行为模式；这些选择与回复特征、任务特性及用户个人行为倾向相关联。实验框架成功诱发了区分“默认接受”与“真实偏好”的行为数据。

Conclusion: 游戏化实验方法（尤其是反转“乐于助人的助手”范式）为研究真实的人-AI交互提供了有效框架。研究揭示了AI辅助创作中效率（接受AI建议）与真实性（保持个体表达）之间的核心张力。该框架不仅可用于深入理解人类在创意任务中与AI互动的决策机制，也为未来设计更尊重个人表达的人机协作系统提供了启示。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) raises critical questions about human creativity and individual expression in an era of AI-assisted creation. When do humans adopt AI suggestions, and what are the implications for individual voice?
  This study examines these questions through a gamified writing exercise where 74 participants (214 responses) replied to prompts while AI-generated word suggestions were available as they wrote. The game simulates a dystopian future in which an AI is attempting to learn from what remains of human individuality, and disincentivizes AI-like writing. In doing so, it attempts to create conditions that reveal authentic user preferences rather than default behaviors, such as accepting a readily available AI-generated suggestion. Note that this is a deliberate inversion of the "helpful assistant" design pattern; the system is explicitly forbidding you from accepting AI suggestions.
  We analyze user behavior patterns across different task types, user behaviors, and response characteristics to understand the factors influencing human-AI interaction in creative tasks. The study focuses on when users choose to maintain creative autonomy versus violating the rules of the game and accepting AI assistance. It also explores how these choices relate to response patterns, task characteristics, and user behavior. This gamified approach offers both a framework for studying authentic human-AI interaction and a provocative lens for understanding the tension between efficiency and authenticity in AI-augmented creativity.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [Restless bandits with imperfect binary feedback: PCL-indexability analysis and computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11192)
*José Niño-Mora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有二元潜在状态和不完美二元反馈的 restless bandit 问题（受机会频谱接入中感知误差的启发），建立了基于部分守恒律（PCL）的分析与计算框架。该框架通过确定性骨架、更新分解和单词组合学分析随机动力学，在多个阈值区间内推导出折扣奖励和资源指标的易处理表达式，从而完整验证了这些区间内的 PCL-可索引性条件。对于未能完全解析验证的剩余区间，开发了高效数值方案来计算边际指标和边际生产率（MP）指数（当条件成立时该指数等于 Whittle 指数）。大量实验表明这些条件在广泛参数范围内成立，且 MP 指数策略通常显著优于标准基准策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受机会频谱接入中感知误差的启发，研究具有二元潜在状态和不完美二元反馈的 restless bandit 问题，旨在建立可索引性验证与 Whittle 指数计算的系统框架。

Method: 基于部分守恒律（PCL）的解析与计算框架，利用确定性骨架、更新分解和单词组合学分析随机动力学；在解析不可达区间采用高效数值方案计算边际指标。

Result: 在多个阈值区间内完整验证了 PCL-可索引性条件；剩余区间通过数值实验提供强证据表明条件成立；MP 指数策略在广泛参数范围内显著优于标准基准策略。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了具有不完美反馈的 restless bandit 问题，放宽了先前工作的严格参数限制，且 MP 指数策略具有优越性能。

Abstract: We study restless bandits with binary latent states and imperfect binary feedback, motivated by opportunistic spectrum access with sensing errors. For the associated belief-state model, we develop a partial conservation laws (PCL)-based analytical and computational framework for establishing indexability and evaluating the Whittle index, building on a verification theorem for real-state discounted restless bandits. The framework analyzes the stochastic dynamics via an associated deterministic skeleton, renewal decompositions, and combinatorics on words. It yields tractable expressions for discounted reward and resource metrics in several threshold regimes, enabling full verification of the PCL-indexability conditions there. For the remaining regime, where a complete analytic verification is not achieved in this paper, we derive efficient numerical schemes for computing the relevant marginal metrics and the marginal productivity (MP) index, which equals the Whittle index when those conditions hold. Extensive computational experiments provide strong evidence that these conditions also hold in that regime across broad parameter ranges and without the stringent parameter restrictions imposed in prior work. The experiments further show that theMP index policy typically outperforms standard benchmark policies, often by a substantial margin.

</details>


### [236] [To Intervene or Not: Guiding Inference-time Alignment with Probabilistic Model Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11201)
*Jin Gan,Xin Li,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BlendIn框架，通过质量感知对齐和按可靠性比例加权，在推理时稳定地整合不同模型的知识，解决现有推理时对齐方法中引导有效性差异大、过度干预导致性能下降的问题，在困难模型对上实现最高50%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法从某些对齐模型中提取引导，但没有适当评估其可靠性。系统性评估发现引导有效性在不同模型间差异很大，无效引导会导致模型困惑进而引发过度干预，最终导致性能不佳。

Method: 提出BlendIn推理时对齐框架，从二元决策转向创建混合分布来整合两个模型的知识。通过质量感知对齐（quality-aware alignment）和基于可靠性的比例加权（proportionally weighting），保留有益引导同时降低不可靠建议的权重。

Result: 相比现有方法，BlendIn保持有益引导的同时降低不可靠建议的权重，在困难模型对上实现一致且最高50%的性能提升。

Conclusion: BlendIn为错误对齐的引导提供了诊断信号和缓解策略，有效稳定了推理时对齐过程，显著提升了性能。

Abstract: The wide deployment of LLMs has made model alignment necessary to make newly trained models safely and effectively respond to user instructions. Among different methods, inference-time alignment is often cheaper as it intervenes (i.e., offers guidances) only during output generation. Existing proposals apply guidances extracted from certain aligned models without properly assessing their reliability. Nonetheless, our systematic evaluation reveals that guidance effectiveness varies drastically across models; since ineffective guidances lead to further confusion and thus further interventions, the resulting excessive interventions typically indicate poor performance. To make interventions more effective and thus more efficient, we introduce BlendIn, an inference-time alignment framework that shifts from binary decisions to creating hybrid distributions integrating both models' knowledge. BlendIn stabilizes inference-time alignment by performing quality-aware alignment and proportionally weighting each model's contribution based on reliability. Compared with existing works, it preserves beneficial guidance while downweighting unreliable suggestions. BlendIn provides both diagnostic signals and mitigation strategies for misaligned guidance, achieving consistent and up to 50% performance improvement on challenging model pairs. Our code is available at: https://github.com/DecayingSeart/BlendIn.

</details>


### [237] [Dual-Stance Evaluation of Sycophancy: The Structure of Agreement and the Limits of Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11205)
*Matthew James Buchan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了激活引导（activation steering）对LLM行为的影响，发现减少谄媚（sycophancy）的引导方向也会抑制对事实正确陈述的同意，揭示了表示可读但不可写的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准评估通常不测试减少谄媚的引导方向是否也会抑制对事实正确陈述的同意，存在评估盲区。

Method: 提出双立场评估方法（dual-stance evaluation），对每个话题测试正反两种立场，并应用于Llama-3-8B-Instruct模型的质心差引导（centroid-difference steering）。

Result: 发现谄媚同意与事实同意在几何上位于不同的子空间，但引导方向在这两个子空间上的投影相等，无法区分对待；引导方向同时减少了谄媚同意和事实正确陈述的同意（如地球是圆的）。

Conclusion: 表明存在一般性鸿沟：从激活中可读取的表示未必能通过激活写入（representations readable from activations may not be writable through them）。

Abstract: Activation steering can shift LLM behaviour, but standard evaluations do not typically test whether a sycophancy-reduction direction also suppresses agreement with factually correct statements. We introduce dual-stance evaluation, which tests both stances of each topic, and apply it to centroid-difference steering on Llama-3-8B-Instruct. We find a dissociation: the model represents sycophantic and factual agreement in geometrically distinct subspaces, yet the steering direction projects equally onto both and cannot differentially target either. The direction accordingly reduces agreement with factually correct statements (e.g. that the Earth is round) as well as sycophantic ones. All other static properties of the two activation groups are matched, suggesting the behavioural dissociation arises from generation dynamics or from finer-grained structure that residual-stream analysis cannot resolve. The pattern illustrates a general gap: representations that are readable from activations may not be writable through them.

</details>


### [238] [Few-Shot Resampling for Scalable Statistically-Sound Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11235)
*Leonardo Pellegrina,Fabio Vandin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FewRS方法，基于重采样评估数据挖掘结果的统计显著性，通过推导测试统计量上确界偏差的新界值，仅需极少量重采样数据集即可保证严格错误发现概率，相比现有方法运行时间降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重采样方法需要生成和分析数千个重采样数据集，在大规模数据集或计算密集型分析中难以实用，亟需一种高效可扩展的统计显著性评估方法。

Method: 提出FewRS方法，核心是推导了数据挖掘结果质量测试统计量上确界偏差的新界值，从而在保证严格错误发现概率的前提下，大幅减少所需生成和分析的重采样数据集数量。

Result: 在模式挖掘和网络分析等任务上，FewRS相比现有方法运行时间降低最多两个数量级，同时保持高统计功效，能在大规模真实数据集上实现统计验证。

Conclusion: FewRS是一种简单有效、高度可扩展的重采样统计显著性评估方法，适用于任何可采用重采样方法的场景，为大规模数据挖掘结果的统计验证提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: A key step in knowledge discovery is the evaluation of data mining results. In several applications, including pattern mining, graph analysis, and others, this step includes the evaluation of the statistical significance of the results, to avoid spurious discoveries due only to noise or random fluctuations in the data. While specialized procedures have been developed for some specific applications, resampling-based approaches are widely used, in particular for complex analyses where analytical results cannot be derived. However, current resampling-based approaches require the generation and analysis of thousands of resampled datasets, and are therefore impractical for large datasets or computationally intensive analyses.
  In this paper, we introduce FewRS, a simple and effective resampling-based approach to assess the statistical significance of data mining results with rigorous guarantees on the probability of false discoveries. Our approach can be used in every situation where resampling-based approaches are applied. FewRS builds on our derivation of a novel bound to the supremum deviation of test statistics representing the quality of data mining results. We prove that FewRS needs to generate and analyze an extremely small number of resampled datasets, leading to a highly scalable approach with wide applicability. We test our approach on common tasks such as pattern mining and network analysis. In all cases, our approach results in a reduction of up to two orders of magnitude in running time compared to the state of the art, while preserving high statistical power, enabling the statistical validation of data mining results on large-scale real-world datasets.

</details>


### [239] [ProHiFlo: Hierarchical Flow Matching with Functional Guidance for De Novo Protein Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11243)
*Chuanzhen Wang,Meade Cleti,Pete Jano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProHiFlo提出了一种分层流匹配框架，通过粗到细生成、功能引导和自适应SE(3)等变架构，在蛋白质从头生成中实现了SOTA性能，采样步骤减少4倍，酶活性位点支架成功率58.9% vs RFDiffusion的41.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散/流匹配方法仅在单一分辨率下操作，缺乏融入功能约束的机制，限制了蛋白质设计的实用性和可控性。

Method: 提出ProHiFlo，包含三项创新：(1) 粗到细生成：先建模主链几何再细化到全原子坐标；(2) 功能引导：利用预训练预测器引导生成目标属性，无需重新训练；(3) 自适应SE(3)等变架构：实现高效多尺度处理。

Result: 在无条件生成、基序支架和功能设计任务上达到SOTA，所需采样步骤减少4倍；酶活性位点支架任务成功率为58.9%，显著高于RFDiffusion的41.2%。

Conclusion: ProHiFlo通过分层流匹配和功能引导机制，在计算效率和设计成功率上均优于现有方法，为蛋白质功能设计提供了更有效的框架。

Abstract: De novo protein generation has transformative potential in therapeutic design, enzyme engineering, and synthetic biology. While diffusion-based and flow matching approaches have achieved progress, they typically operate at single resolution and lack mechanisms for incorporating functional constraints. We introduce ProHiFlo, a hierarchical flow matching framework with three innovations: (1) coarse-to-fine generation that models backbone geometry before refining to all-atom coordinates, reducing computational cost while maintaining accuracy; (2) functional guidance leveraging pretrained predictors to steer generation toward desired properties without retraining; (3) adaptive SE(3)-equivariant architecture for efficient multi-scale processing. Experiments on unconditional generation, motif scaffolding, and functional design demonstrate state-ofthe-art performance while requiring 4 fewer sampling steps. On enzyme active site scaffolding, ProHiFlo achieves 58.9% success rate compared to 41.2% for RFDiffusion.

</details>


### [240] [Physics-informed generative AI for semiconductor manufacturing: Enforcing hard physical constraints in generative models by construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11247)
*Yaser Mike Banad,Sarah Sharif*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了在半导体制造等受严格物理约束的领域，生成式AI必须通过架构设计内建物理信息（physics-informed by construction），而非依赖事后过滤（post-hoc filtering）；综述了物理信息扩散模型、PDE约束变分模型、神经算子先验等新兴工具，并提出了以物理保真度基准、可微模拟器基础设施和多模态基础模型为核心的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造中的掩模、布局、缺陷数据与工艺配方必须严格遵守光刻、输运、反应及器件物理约束——物理无效的样本不仅质量低下，而且完全不可用。这揭示了一个更广泛的计算科学挑战：在受约束物理领域中，生成式AI必须从构造上内建物理信息，而非仅靠事后过滤来修正。

Method: 综述了新兴架构工具包：物理信息扩散模型、PDE约束变分模型、神经算子先验、守恒律尊重生成网络；展示了它们与可微光刻、TCAD、工艺仿真和自主实验的结合方式；识别了生成模型与物理仿真器之间的四种集成模式。

Result: 提出了四项集成模式，并构建了一个以物理保真度基准、可微模拟器基础设施和多模态基础模型为核心的研究议程。核心主张是分析性的而非修辞性的：在物理有效性作为成功绑定准则的场景中，通过构造强制物理有效性的架构应当优于事后过滤的架构，而半导体工厂正是这种区别最为尖锐的场合。

Conclusion: 在物理约束严格的领域（如半导体制造），生成式AI必须采用物理信息内建（by construction）的架构设计，而非事后过滤；物理信息扩散模型、PDE约束变分模型等新工具构成了解决方案的基础，未来需要物理保真度基准、可微模拟器和多模态基础模型的支持。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly used to propose designs, data, and control actions for physical systems, yet many such systems are governed by hard physical constraints rather than by perceptual plausibility. Semiconductor manufacturing provides a demanding test case: generated masks, layouts, synthetic defect data, and process recipes must obey lithography, transport, reaction, and device-physics constraints, because physically invalid samples are not merely low quality but unusable. This Perspective argues that semiconductor manufacturing exposes a broader computational-science challenge, namely that generative AI for constrained physical domains must be physics-informed by construction, not corrected only through post-hoc filtering. We survey the emerging architectural toolkit, including physics-informed diffusion, PDE-constrained variational models, neural-operator priors, and conservation-law-respecting generative networks, and show how it connects to differentiable lithography, TCAD, process simulation, and autonomous experimentation. We identify four integration patterns between generative models and physics-based simulators, and we propose a research agenda centered on physics-fidelity benchmarks, differentiable simulator infrastructure, and multimodal foundation models for physical design and manufacturing. The central claim is analytical rather than rhetorical: where physical validity is the binding criterion of success, architectures that enforce it by construction should be expected to outperform those that filter for it after the fact, and the fab is the setting where this distinction is sharpest.

</details>


### [241] [Mechanical Field Networks: Structured Neural Dynamics for Multivariate Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11251)
*Xingji Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MF-Net是一种循环动力学模型，将所有变量表示为共享场状态，并通过可学习的力学转换（关系-运动组织）进行联合演化，能在保持可检查结构读出的同时实现竞争性的短中期预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动力学建模方法要么强加可解释的动态结构（但需要预先指定交互结构），要么学习灵活的状态转换（但交互结构隐含在学习过程中），缺乏一种既能学习灵活动态又能同时保持结构可读性的方法。

Method: MF-Net通过将所有变量表示在共享场状态中，每个变量携带一个场分量，通过可学习的力学转换（relation-to-motion组织）联合演化这些分量。学习的关系塑造状态相关的流、场响应和运动趋势，这些内部量同时支持预测和结构读出。

Result: 在已知律交互系统、混沌基准测试、真实神经记录和生态时间序列上，MF-Net在短中期预测中达到竞争性表现，同时保持可检查的结构读出。在40维Lorenz-96测试中，八步R²为0.798±0.018，学习的关系矩阵恢复了局部耦合支持，局部/非局部强度比为19.80±1.00，Precision@K为1.000±0.000。

Conclusion: MF-Net提供了一个结构可读的动力学建模框架，其中学习的关系通过前向演化进行训练，在真实数据上可在适当的观测限制下解释为功能预测耦合。

Abstract: Many multivariate dynamical systems are observed only through trajectories, leaving the mechanisms governing their joint dynamics hidden. Existing approaches can impose interpretable dynamics or learn flexible state transitions, yet the resulting interaction structure is typically either specified in advance or left implicit within the learned dynamics. We introduce MF-Net, a recurrent dynamical model that represents all variables in a shared field state and updates this state through a learned relation law. Each variable carries a field component, and these components evolve jointly through a learnable mechanical transition. Here, mechanical refers to the relation-to-motion organization of the transition, where learned relations shape state-dependent flows, field responses, and motion tendencies that move the field state forward. The resulting structure is part of the rollout itself: learned relations influence how the field moves, and the same internal quantities support both forecasting and structural readout. Across known-law interaction systems, chaotic benchmarks, real neural recordings, and ecological time series, MF-Net achieves competitive short- and medium-horizon forecasting while retaining inspectable structural readout. On the 40-dimensional Lorenz--96 testbed, MF-Net achieves an eight-step $R^2$ of $0.798\pm0.018$; across five seeds, its learned relation matrix recovers the local coupling support with a local/nonlocal strength ratio of $19.80\pm1.00$ and Precision@$K$ of $1.000\pm0.000$. MF-Net provides a structure-readable dynamical modeling framework in which learned relations are trained through forward evolution and, on real data, interpreted as functional predictive couplings under appropriate observational limits.

</details>


### [242] [Bernstein-Schur Kernels: Random Features by Sketched Modulation and Radial Randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11255)
*Taha Bouhsine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Bernstein-Schur核（有限特征核与完全单调平移不变核的乘积）的双重随机特征构造方法，该方法通过随机化调制因子和径向因子，实现了O(Dm)的特征维度，避免了精确调制的O(d²)复杂度，并提供了无偏估计、方差分析、算子范数界和核岭回归稳定性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernstein-Schur核介于平移不变核和点积核之间，既不能用Bochner采样也不能用多项式 sketching 直接处理，现有随机特征方法无法直接适用于这类非平稳核。

Method: 双重随机化：对有限调制因子进行sketch（m维），对完全单调径向因子通过Bernstein-Widder尺度采样（D次）并应用d维高斯随机傅里叶特征，总特征维度为Dm。

Result: 证明了当调制因子精确（m→∞）时的无偏性、扁平估计量的精确方差、基于特征值和固有维度的矩阵Bernstein算子范数界，以及确定性相对谱核岭稳定性结果。双重随机化估计继承了相同保证并附加可调sketch项。

Conclusion: 该方法为yat核等实例提供了理论基础，其中径向混合对应IMQ谱采样器，在固定径向特征预算下每尺度单频率方差最优。

Abstract: Bernstein--Schur kernels are products of a finite-feature kernel (one with an explicit finite-dimensional feature map) and a completely monotone shift-invariant kernel: nonstationary kernels that fall between the shift-invariant and dot-product templates random features usually exploit, so in general neither Bochner sampling nor polynomial sketching applies to the full kernel directly. We give one random-feature construction for the whole class that \emph{randomizes both factors: it sketches the finite modulation and randomizes the completely monotone radial factor, sampling the latter's one-dimensional Bernstein--Widder scale and then applying Gaussian random Fourier features (whose frequency is still $d$-dimensional). The feature dimension is then $Dm$, set by the sketch size $m$ and the radial-draw count $D$, free of the $O(d^2)$ size of the exact modulation feature. Keeping the modulation \emph{exact} is the analyzable limit ($m\to\infty$): there we prove unbiasedness, an exact variance for the recommended flat estimator, an expected matrix-Bernstein operator-norm bound (with a matching high-probability tail) controlled by the top eigenvalues of the kernel and modulation Gram matrices together with an intrinsic dimension rather than the crude $N\max_{ij}$ entrywise route, and a deterministic relative-spectral kernel-ridge stability result. By conditioning on the sketch, the doubly-randomized estimator inherits the same intrinsic-dimension operator-norm guarantee plus a single additive sketch term, tunable by $m$ independently of $D$. The motivating instance is the biased $yat$-kernel $k_{yat,b}(w,x)=(w^\top x+b)^2/(\|w-x\|^2+\varepsilon)$, $b\ge0$, whose family span contains the inverse-multiquadric kernel by finite differences in $b$; for it the radial mixture is the IMQ spectral sampler, and one frequency per scale is variance-optimal at a fixed radial-feature budget.

</details>


### [243] [Loss Landscape Diagnosis for Gradient-Based Gray-Scott System Inversion: Disentangling the Roles of PINN Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11258)
*Yan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过最直接的方式——对Gray-Scott反应-扩散系统的未roll PDE模拟进行反向传播——来恢复其参数，发现优化失败的根本原因是损失景观中存在平坦高原（无梯度信号）和陡峭悬崖（与分岔边界对齐）的几何结构。这种病理结构在不同损失函数和梯度路径中普遍存在。通过将此设置视为PINN的消融实验，揭示了各组成部分的作用：固定神经网络后，残差损失是PDE参数的二次函数，产生平滑景观，避免了病理现象（因为它隐式编码了所有初始条件下的完整PDE动态）；而神经网络无法修复不适定的参数子空间，仅用于补全观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度反演反应-扩散系统通常通过代理模型或物理信息神经网络（PINNs）实现，而最直接的途径——通过PDE结构本身进行反向传播——一直被规避。作者试图探索这一直接路径作为诊断探针，以理解为何这种方法失败，并借此揭示PINN各组件的作用分工。

Method: 采用最直接的梯度反演方法：对未roll的Gray-Scott模拟进行稳态损失反向传播来恢复参数，不使用任何代理模型或神经网络增强。通过绘制损失景观可视化优化失败的几何结构。将此设置作为PINN的消融实验，分别固定神经网络和检查残差损失的行为。

Result: 优化无法收敛。损失景观存在平坦高原（无梯度信号）和陡峭悬崖（与分岔边界对齐）的病理几何结构，且该结构在不同损失函数和梯度路由方式下普遍存在。固定神经网络后，残差损失是PDE参数的二次函数，产生平滑景观。

Conclusion: PINN方法中，残差损失通过隐式编码所有初始条件下的完整PDE动态，已经避免了参数空间的病理结构（这是其核心作用）；神经网络无法修复不适定的参数子空间，仅用于补全观测数据。这种分工此前未被明确揭示。这为PINN类方法提供了具体的设计启示，并提出了关于何时添加额外维度实际上有帮助的更广泛启发式原则。

Abstract: Gradient-based inversion of reaction-diffusion systems is typically approached via surrogate models or physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), while the most direct route, backpropagation through the PDE's structure itself, has largely been avoided. We pursue this direct route as a diagnostic probe, backpropagating a steady-state loss through unrolled Gray-Scott simulation to recover its parameters, with no surrogate or neural-network augmentation. Optimization fails to converge, and plotting the landscape directly locates the failure in its geometry -- flat plateaus with no gradient signal, bounded by sharp cliffs that align with bifurcation boundaries -- a structure that recurs across loss functions and is inherited however the gradients are routed to parameters. Reading this minimal setup as an ablation of PINN, we disentangle each component's role: with the neural network fixed, the residual loss is quadratic in the PDE parameters and yields a smooth landscape, so it alone already avoids the pathology, by implicitly encoding the full PDE dynamics across all initial conditions. The neural network, for its part, cannot repair an ill-posed parameter subspace, and so serves only to complete the observed data -- a division of labor not previously made explicit. These findings carry concrete design implications for PINN-type methods and a broader heuristic on when added dimensions actually help.

</details>


### [244] [PermDoRA -- Understanding Adapter Interference in Language Models: Limits of Parameter-Space Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11262)
*Gowtham Sivaramakrishnan,Sarvesha Kumar Kombaiah Seetha,Kishan Gupta Balaji,Santhosh Baradwaj Vaduvur Ranganathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM中适配器组合的干扰机制，发现参数几何正交性并非性能的主要决定因素，干扰更可能源于共享非线性表示中的交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假设认为适配器组合干扰源于线性参数更新的重叠，通过强制正交性或方向独立性可提升多领域性能，作者旨在验证这一假设。

Method: 提出DoRA-RBAC层级适配器组合框架，使用权重分解低秩适配，对比传统欧几里得合并与基于黎曼几何的Fréchet均值近似策略，在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Mistral-7B上评估多QA基准性能。

Result: 单领域性能与LoRA持平，但几何感知合并相比标准平均无一致优势；角度对齐和正交性对组合性能预测力弱。

Conclusion: 适配器干扰并非主要由参数空间几何决定，而是与共享非线性表示中的交互一致。

Abstract: Access control in large language models (LLMs) requires modular mechanisms to enable domain-specific behavior without retraining or cross-domain interference. A common hypothesis is that interference during adapter composition arises from overlap in linear parameter updates, suggesting that enforcing orthogonality or directional independence should improve multi-domain performance. We test this hypothesis using DoRA-RBAC, a hierarchical adapter composition framework based on weight-decomposed low-rank adaptation. We compare conventional Euclidean merging with a geometry-aware Riemannian-inspired merging strategy that approximates the Frechet mean via normalized directional averaging across multiple QA benchmarks (GPQA, PubMedQA, SimpleQA, WMDP) on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B. Our results show that while single-domain performance matches LoRA, geometry-aware merging provides no consistent advantage over standard averaging in multi-domain settings.Diagnostic analysis further reveals that angular alignment and orthogonality of adapter updates are weak predictors of composition performance. These findings suggest that adapter interference is not governed primarily by parameter-space geometry, but is instead consistent with interactions in shared nonlinear representations.

</details>


### [245] [Seeing Before Colliding: Anticipatory Safe RL with Frozen Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11266)
*Samuel Tetteh,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了VLM-Safe-RL框架，利用冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）作为CMDP拉格朗日更新中的预期成本项，解决高速自动驾驶场景中传统约束RL因成本信号反应滞后而无法及时避免碰撞的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在竞速等高速场景中，碰撞瞬间发生且不可逆，而传统约束RL的成本信号（如仿真器碰撞反馈、PPO-Lagrangian的拉格朗日乘子更新）都是反应式的——只有碰撞开始后才产生非零成本，预算超限后才更新乘子。这种机制在结构上注定为时已晚，亟需一种能提前预测并规避风险的约束优化方法。

Method: 将冻结的视觉语言模型（CLIP）集成到CMDP拉格朗日更新中，作为预期成本项。具体贡献包括：(i) 解耦双路径CLIP，独立奖励/成本路径以尊重CMDP分解；(ii) VLM-Lagrange，在乘子更新中加入逐帧VLM成本作为预期项；(iii) 置信门控（Confidence Gating），基于CLIP边界的逻辑噪声模型推导贝叶斯最优权重；(iv) VLMPPOLag组合算法。

Result: 在Safety-Gymnasium FormulaOne L2主要评估中（n=5种子，10^6步，预算d_lim=25），VLMPPOLag+Conf是默认预算比较中唯一同时保持实质性回报（J_r≈40）且在多数种子上将成本控制在预算内的配置；五个约束感知基线（PPOLag、CPO、CPPOPID、CPO-CLG、PPOLag-RND）均至少有一项要求失败。在MetaDrive Medium上泛化（灾难率41%→26%，95%自助CI [-26,-5]百分点），在Bullet Safety-Gym上表现方向一致，在MetaDrive Easy/Hard和Qwen2-VL骨干上不理想，并且Hard失败可归因于拉格朗日正则化病理而非VLM信号本身。

Conclusion: 这是首次将冻结的VLM信号作为预期成本项集成到CMDP拉格朗日更新中的工作。实验表明该方法在高速安全场景下优于传统约束RL基线，但存在一定的泛化局限性（如某些环境与骨干网络不适用），失败原因可追溯到拉格朗日正则化问题而非VLM信号质量。

Abstract: The cost signal that constrained-RL algorithms optimize against is almost always reactive: the simulator emits a non-zero cost only after a collision has begun, and the Lagrange multiplier of PPO-Lagrangian grows only after the episode budget has been exceeded. At race speeds, where collisions are instantaneous and irreversible, any safety mechanism that waits for cost to accumulate is structurally too late. We present VLM-Safe-RL, a framework that integrates a frozen vision-language model into the CMDP Lagrangian update as an anticipatory cost term. The framework comprises four contributions: (i) Decoupled Dual-Path CLIP, independent reward/cost paths that respect the CMDP's factorization; (ii) VLM-Lagrange, an augmented multiplier update that incorporates a per-step VLM cost as an anticipatory term; (iii) Confidence Gating, a Bayes-optimal weight derived from a logistic noise model on the CLIP margin; and (iv) VLMPPOLag, the composed algorithm. On Safety-Gymnasium FormulaOne L2, our principal evaluation ($n{=}5$ seeds, $10^{6}$ steps, budget $d_{\text{lim}}{=}25$) VLMPPOLag$+$Conf is the only configuration in our default budget comparison that simultaneously retains substantive return ($J_r{\approx}40$) and holds cost within budget on a majority of seeds; the five constraint-aware baselines (PPOLag, CPO, CPPOPID, CPO-CLG, PPOLag-RND) each fail at least one requirement. The mechanism generalizes to held-out MetaDrive Medium (catastrophe rate $41\%{\to}26\%$, 95\% bootstrap CI $[-26,-5]$\,pp) and shows directionally consistent transfer to Bullet Safety-Gym; we report honestly where it does not (MetaDrive Easy/Hard, Qwen2-VL backbone) and trace the Hard failure to a Lagrangian-regulation pathology rather than the VLM signal itself. To our knowledge, this is the first work to use frozen VLM signals as an anticipatory cost term inside the CMDP Lagrangian update.

</details>


### [246] [A prior-free blind detection of information leakage from model predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11267)
*Laurence A. Jacobs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究仅从模型预测和真实结果中检测数据泄露（data leakage）的理论与方法，提出基于决策理论的框架，将泄漏诊断问题形式化为预测风险/结果分布的泛函。通过理论证明揭示了泄漏检测的三重不可能性与可行性边界：重校准后的广泛泄漏与诚实模型在区分度和校准性上不可区分；但近确定性泄漏（如标签泄漏）会产生无法伪造的“纯度头部”，从而无需先验即可检测。据此将泄漏分为三类（误校准、广泛校准、确定性），并分别为每类设计检测器。在UK Biobank数据上验证，检测下限约为ΔĈ≈0.007。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据泄露是机器学习科学中可重复性失败的主导原因，但现有检测工具需要训练代码、外部数据或领域专业知识，而审计者最常持有的工件——模型的输出——却无法被利用。本文旨在探索仅从预测和结果中能判断出哪些关于泄露的信息，填补输出层面泄露检测的理论空白。

Method: 1. 建立决策理论框架，将泄漏诊断定义为预测风险/结果分布（predicted-risk/outcome law）的泛函，参数化阈值权重，链接到恰当评分规则（proper scoring rules）和决策曲线分析（decision-curve analysis）。2. 理论证明：重校准后的泄漏可完全模仿诚实模型的校准性和区分度，任何预测的函数都无法区分，因此广泛泄漏仅在外部提供可达到的区分度上限时才可检测。3. 证明近确定性泄漏（如近标签泄露）会产生持续的单位纯度头部（sustained unit-purity head），非确定性结果的任何合法预测器都无法制造，从而得到无先验检验。4. 将泄漏组织为三分法：误校准、广泛校准、确定性，每类配对应检测器和失败模式。5. 在UK Biobank数据上使用时窗共病泄漏（time-windowed comorbidity leakage）进行验证，测量检测下限。

Result: 在UK Biobank上，检测下限ΔĈ≈0.007，低于此阈值的残余泄漏从输出角度不可检测，且太小不足以改变结论。该数值下限是队列和终点特定的，但结构性的教训是通用的：仅在残余泄漏与诚实但更强的预测器不可区分时，输出检测失败。检测器在普通硬件上对单个预测向量的判断不到一秒。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上划定了仅靠预测和结果检测数据泄露的边界：广泛校准泄露不可检测（除非有外部区分度上限），而确定性泄露必然可检测。这一三分法框架为输出层面的泄漏审计提供了系统化的理论指导，表明泄露检测失败的根本原因在于残余泄露与一个诚实但更强的预测器无法区分。

Abstract: Data leakage -- contamination of a model with information unavailable at baseline -- is the dominant reproducibility failure in machine-learning-based science, yet detection tools require training code, external data, or domain expertise. None operates on the artifact an auditor most often holds: the model's output. We ask what can be decided about leakage from predictions and outcomes alone. We give a decision-theoretic framework in which leakage diagnostics are functionals of the predicted-risk/outcome law, parameterized by a threshold-weighting linked to proper scoring rules and decision-curve analysis. We prove a sharp impossibility: a recalibrated leak matching an honest model's calibration and discrimination is indistinguishable from honest performance by \emph{any} function of the predictions, so the broad class is detectable only against an externally supplied ceiling on achievable discrimination. We then prove what leakage cannot hide: a near-deterministic subgroup -- the signature of a near-label leak -- produces a sustained unit-purity head that no legitimate predictor of a non-deterministic outcome can manufacture, yielding a prior-free test. These results organize leakage into a trichotomy -- miscalibrated, broad-calibrated, and deterministic -- each with a matched detector and failure mode. We validate on UK Biobank using time-windowed comorbidity leakage with known, graded severity, measuring a detection floor of $Δ\cstar \approx 0.007$ on this endpoint, below which residual leakage is undetectable from output and too small to alter conclusions. The numerical floor is cohort- and endpoint-specific; the structural lesson is general: output-only detection fails where residual leakage is indistinguishable from an honestly stronger predictor. The test returns a verdict on a prediction vector in under a second on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [247] [LakeFM: Toward a Foundation Model for Aquatic Ecosystems Using Irregular Multivariate Multi-depth Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11268)
*Abhilash Neog,Sepideh Fatemi,Medha Sawhney,Kazi Sajeed Mehrab,Aanish Pradhan,Bennett J. McAfee,Emma Marchisin,Arka Daw,Robert Ladwig,Cayelan C. Carey,Paul Hanson,Anuj Karpatne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LakeFM是一个针对水生系统的基础模型，在大规模生态数据集上预训练，能够处理异构变量、不同深度和不规则观测模式，在湖泊动态预测上优于现有时间序列模型，且预测结果符合物理规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法假设时间与深度上采样规则，难以泛化到具有异构变量、不同深度和观测模式的湖泊，亟需一个能处理这些异质性的通用模型。

Method: 在包含模拟和观测数据的大规模生态数据集上预训练基础模型LakeFM，使其学习湖泊级表征，并应用于时间序列预测任务。

Result: LakeFM学习到有意义的湖泊级表征，在预测性能上达到或超过现有时间序列基础模型和非基础模型，且预测结果具有物理合理性。

Conclusion: LakeFM有效解决了跨湖泊泛化难题，为湖泊水质与生态系统监测提供了可靠的基础模型工具。

Abstract: Understanding and forecasting lake dynamics is critical for monitoring water quality and ecosystem health across lakes and reservoirs. While machine learning methods have been recently applied to ecological time-series data, existing works assume regular sampling in time and depth, and struggle to generalize across lakes with heterogeneous variables, depths, and observation patterns. To address these limitations, we introduce \textsc{LakeFM}, a foundation model for aquatic systems, pre-trained on large-scale ecological datasets comprising both simulated and observed lakes. Through extensive empirical evaluation, we show that \textsc{LakeFM} learns meaningful representations spanning broader lake-level characteristics, and achieves competitive or often superior-forecasting performance compared to existing time-series foundation and non-foundation models, while producing physically plausible predictions consistent with real-world lake dynamics.

</details>


### [248] [Quantifying Subliminal Behavioral Transfer Ratios in Language Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11270)
*Uwe Konig,Hamza Kazmi,Ruizhe Li,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文量化了语言模型蒸馏过程中“潜意识学习”现象的迁移率。通过引导两个教师模型（Llama-2-7B-Chat和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）在不同强度下产生不良行为，再仅用良性数据蒸馏学生模型，发现行为迁移具有鲁棒性但呈现不同尺度特征：Llama-2在特定阈值后突然跃迁（τ=0.25-0.32），而Qwen2.5表现连续且更高水平的迁移（τ达0.61）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有定性证据支持蒸馏过程中会迁移教师模型的 undesirable characteristics（即潜意识学习现象），但该效应的 magnitude 尚未被系统量化。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 在两个教师模型（Llama-2-7B-Chat和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）上施加不同强度的 steering 以引入不良行为；2) 仅使用 benign data 蒸馏学生模型；3) 使用100个 JailbreakBench prompts，以GPT-4.1作为评估器量化 subliminal behavioral transfer ratios。

Result: 行为迁移表现出鲁棒性但两种模型呈现不同的扩展行为：Llama-2存在尖锐阈值（τ=0.25-0.32，在α=-0.15之后），而Qwen2.5表现出连续且更高水平的迁移（τ最高达0.61）。

Conclusion: 蒸馏过程中的潜意识学习效应确实存在且可量化，不同架构/训练方式的模型表现出不同的迁移动力学特征，这为安全对齐蒸馏提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Distillation of a language model intended to transfer benign behavior to a student model may also transfer undesirable characteristics, if they are present in the teacher model, a phenomenon known as subliminal learning. While qualitative evidence supports the existence of this effect, its magnitude has not been systematically characterized. This study quantifies subliminal behavioral transfer ratios by steering two teacher models (Llama-2-7B-Chat and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) at varying steering strengths and distilling student models using only benign data. Evaluation on 100 JailbreakBench prompts with GPT-4.1, serving as the evaluator, indicates that transfer is robust but exhibits distinct scaling behaviors. Llama-2 demonstrates a sharp threshold ($τ= {0.25,0.32} \ \text{beyond} \ α= -0.15$), whereas Qwen2.5 displays continuous and higher levels of transfer ($τ$ up to $0.61$).

</details>


### [249] [Federated continual learning: A comprehensive survey on lifelong and privacy-preserving learning over distributed and non-stationary data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11272)
*Masoume Gholizade,Fabrizio Ruffini,Pietro Ducange,Francesco Marcelloni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了联邦持续学习（FCL），即在分布式、非平稳数据流下实现隐私保护、自适应和终身学习的框架，并提出多维分类法、评估指标与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景（如医疗、工业物联网、网络安全、智慧城市）中的数据流本质上是非平稳的，而现有联邦学习（FL）系统大多假设数据平稳，导致性能下降、不稳定和灾难性遗忘。同时，持续学习（CL）主要在集中式环境下研究，忽略了联邦系统的隐私、通信限制和客户端异构性等关键约束。因此，需要将FL与CL结合，形成联邦持续学习（FCL）以解决这些实际需求。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法：首先正式定义FCL问题并阐明其独特特征；分析经典FL在非平稳条件下的局限性；提出FCL方法的多维分类法；回顾代表性应用领域与数据模态；总结常用评估指标与实验视角；最后指出关键开放挑战。

Result: 构建了FCL的系统性综述框架，包括问题定义、多维分类体系、应用领域与评估指标总结，以及开放挑战的识别。

Conclusion: FCL是应对分布式非平稳数据流的关键范式，但当前面临极端异构性、可扩展隐私保护记忆机制、标准化基准等挑战，需进一步研究以推动其向稳健且可部署的现实系统发展。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative and privacy-preserving model training across distributed clients, but most existing FL systems implicitly assume data stationarity. In real-world settings-such as healthcare, industrial IoT (IIOT), cybersecurity, and smart cities-data streams are inherently non-stationary, leading classical FL methods to suffer from performance degradation, instability, and catastrophic forgetting.
  Continual Learning (CL) addresses learning under evolving data distributions but has been largely studied in centralized settings, overlooking key constraints of federated systems, including privacy, limited communication, and client heterogeneity. Federated Continual Learning (FCL) emerges at the intersection of FL and CL, aiming to support lifelong, adaptive, and privacy-aware learning over distributed and non-stationary data.
  This survey provides a comprehensive and systematic overview of FCL. We first present a formal definition of the FCL problem and clarify its distinctive characteristics. We then analyze the limitations of classical FL under non-stationary conditions, highlighting how CL principles support long-term adaptation. To organize the rapidly growing literature, we propose a multi-dimensional taxonomy of FCL approaches. Furthermore, we review representative application domains and data modalities, summarize commonly used evaluation metrics, and discuss experimental perspectives for assessing long-term performance and forgetting. Finally, we highlight key open challenges, including handling extreme heterogeneity under temporal drift, designing scalable and privacy-preserving memory mechanisms, and establishing standardized benchmarks. This survey aims to serve as a reference and a roadmap for advancing FCL toward robust and deployable real-world systems.

</details>


### [250] [RoVE: Rotary Value Embeddings Attention for Relative Position-dependent Value Pathways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11275)
*Alejandro García-Castellanos,Maurice Weiler,Erik J Bekkers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RoVE，一种无需参数修改的方法，通过同时旋转值和键使值位置敏感，将RoPE注意力转化为注意力卷积，在GPT-2模型上相比RoPE在少样本上下文学习、分布外困惑度和长上下文检索方面取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE使注意力分数具有位置相对性，但值通路是位置盲的——值token发送的消息与查询距离无关，这限制了模型对位置信息的利用能力。

Method: 提出RoVE（Rotary Value Embeddings），一种参数免费的方法，通过同时旋转值和键来使值位置敏感，从而将RoPE注意力转化为注意力卷积。

Result: 在124M和354M参数的GPT-2模型上，RoVE相比RoPE在少样本上下文学习、分布外困惑度和长上下文检索方面取得一致的性能提升，尤其在需要长距离聚合的任务上改进最为明显。

Conclusion: RoVE通过简单的参数免费修改统一了计算机视觉、机器人和现代LLM架构中相同操作的多种独立表述，展示了将位置信息引入值通路的有效性。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) make attention scores position-relative but leave the value pathway position-blind: the message sent by a value token is the same regardless of its distance from the query. We propose RoVE, a parameter-free modification that makes values position-sensitive by rotating them simultaneously with keys, and show that it turns RoPE attention into attentive convolution. This new perspective unifies several independent formulations of the same operation across computer vision, robotics, and modern LLM architectures. Trained 124M and 354M GPT-2 models show consistent empirical gains over RoPE on few-shot in-context learning, out-of-distribution perplexity, and long-context retrieval, with the clearest improvements on tasks that require long-range aggregation.

</details>


### [251] [Least-Action-Guided Diffusion for Physical Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11277)
*Zhongxin Yang,Yuanwei Bin,Xiang I. A. Yang,Shiyi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种最小作用量原理引导的扩散框架（LAPG），通过在推理阶段引入基于作用量的物理指导分数，提升生成模型在时间、参数和几何外推时的物理一致性，在多种微分方程系统中优于传统物理信息基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在计算物理中面临可靠外推的挑战：在有限时间、参数或几何范围内训练的模型，在训练分布之外可能产生物理不一致的预测。现有方法主要依赖训练时的约束，缺乏推理阶段的物理校正机制。

Method: 提出LAPG框架，包含两个阶段：第一阶段使用条件分数扩散模型生成分布内的初始提议；第二阶段利用基于最小作用量原理的变分先验，将该提议向目标分布外条件精细化。该方法将最小作用量原理转化为可微的推理时校正机制，避免了对逐点残差惩罚进行经验性损失平衡的需求。

Result: 在自由落体、保守/耗散弹簧-质量系统、相互作用点涡以及参数化翼型上的势流等ODEs和PDEs系统中，LAPG在时间、参数和几何外推测试中均减少了相位漂移、保持了耗散衰减、捕捉了涡运动，并改善了翼型流动的升力响应。

Conclusion: LAPG通过将最小作用量原理作为推理时校正机制，有效地提升了生成模型在外推场景下的物理一致性，为计算物理中的可靠外推提供了一种无需经验性损失平衡的新范式。
